`thisPlugin.getData/setData/removeData()` - per-product plugin data\n    - `thisPlugin.objects.list/save/delete/get()` - custom object CRUD\n  - `host-app.ts` - Host facade:\n    - `hostApp.getProducts()`, `.getProduct()`, `.getPlugins()`, `.fetch()`\n  - `messaging.ts` - sendMessageAndWait / sendFireAndForget with validation\n\n**Shared SDK**:\n- `/app/plugins/sdk.ts` - **Single source of truth** for all plugins:\n  ```typescript\n  export interface PluginContext { extensionPoint, pluginId, pluginName, hostOrigin, productId? }\n  export interface PluginObject { id, pluginId, objectType, objectId, data, entityType?, entityId? }\n  export interface Product { id, name, sku, description, price, categoryId }\n  export function getSDK(): { thisPlugin, hostApp }\n  ```\n\n**Reference Plugins**:\n\n1. **Warehouse** (`/app/plugins/warehouse/`):\n   - Extension points: menu.main (sidebar), product.detail.tabs, product.list.filters (boolean), product.detail.info\n   - Stores: warehouses (custom objects), stock (composite key {productId}-{warehouseId})\n   - Uses entity binding for product filtering\n\n2. **Box Size** (`/app/plugins/box-size/`):\n   - Extension points: product.detail.tabs, product.detail.info (badge)\n   - Stores: box dimensions (L×W×H) per product via `thisPlugin.setData()`\n   - Simpler pattern: per-product data, no custom objects\n\n**Extension Point Types**:\n- `menu.main` - Sidebar navigation menu item (renders full-page plugin in iframe)\n- `product.detail.tabs` - Tab on product detail page (receives productId)\n- `product.list.filters` - Native filter UI (no iframe; filterKey + filterType in manifest)\n- `product.detail.info` - Inline badge/info below product details (compact ~60px)\n\n---\n\n### 4. CONFIGURATION FILES & FEATURE GATES\n\n**Environment Configuration**:\n- `/app/src/main/resources/application.properties` - Minimal (only app.name=aj)\n- `/app/litellm/config.yaml` - LiteLLM model registry:\n  - gpt-4o-mini (OpenAI)\n  - claude-sonnet-4-20250514 (Anthropic)\n  - Store prompts in spend logs, callbacks to Langfuse\n- `/app/litellm/.env.example` - LLM API keys (OPENAI_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_API_KEY)\n- `/app/plugins/ai-description/.env.example` - LITELLM_API_KEY (for plugin use)\n\n**Database Migrations** (`/app/src/main/resources/db/changelog/2026/`):\n- `001-create-categories-table.yaml`\n- `002-create-products-table.yaml`\n- `003-insert-sample-data.yaml`\n- `004-create-plugins-table.yaml` - Plugin descriptors\n- `005-add-plugin-data-to-products.yaml` - pluginData JSONB column\n- `006-create-plugin-objects-table.yaml` - PluginObject table with entity binding\n- `007-add-entity-binding-to-plugin-objects.yaml` - entity_type, entity_id columns\n\n**Observability Stack** (Docker):\n- **Langfuse** (port 3100) - LLM observability, prompt logging\n- **Presidio Analyzer** (port 5001) + **Anonymizer** (port 5002) - PII detection/filtering (integrated with LiteLLM)\n- **LiteLLM** (port 4000) - Unified LLM proxy with guardrails\n- **Redis** (port 6379) - Langfuse cache\n- **ClickHouse** (ports 8123, 9000) - Analytics\n- **MinIO** (ports 9010, 9011) - S3-compatible storage for Langfuse events\n\n**Feature Gates/Toggles**:\n- **Plugin Enable/Disable**: PATCH `/api/plugins/{pluginId}/enabled` with { enabled: boolean }\n  - Stored in `PluginDescriptor.enabled` column\n  - Only enabled plugins returned by GET /api/plugins\n  - Test patterns in `PluginRegistryIntegrationTests.java`, `PluginObjectGapTests.java`\n- **No dedicated feature gate system** - uses plugin enabled flag + plugin objects for feature data\n\n**Security & Sandboxing**:\n- Plugin iframes sandboxed: `allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-popups allow-modals allow-downloads`\n- Origin validation in messaging system\n- pluginId validation: `^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$`\n- JSONB filter validation: jsonPath alphanumeric/underscore/dot/hyphen only\n- Filter operators constrained: eq, gt, lt, exists, bool\n- PluginDataService validates plugin exists before accessing product data\n\n---\n\n### 5. AI-assisted development artifacts (research, design, implementation logs)\n\n---\n\n### Summary\n\n**AJ** is a production-grade plugin architecture showcase demonstrating:\n- **Microkernel pattern** with Spring Boot host + independent plugin apps\n- **Type-safe plugin SDK** with shared TypeScript types across all plugins\n- **Flexible data storage**: per-product JSONB + custom plugin objects with entity binding\n- **Advanced JSONB querying** with jOOQ for filtered searches\n- **LLM integration** via BAML (structured prompting) + LiteLLM (API management) + Langfuse (observability)\n- **Security**: sandboxed iframes, origin validation, PII filtering via Presidio\n- **Enterprise observability**: Langfuse traces, prompt logging, analytics pipeline (ClickHouse)\n- **Extensibility**: manifest-driven extension points, no code coupling between host and plugins\n\nAll plugins follow consistent patterns defined in `/app/plugins/CLAUDE.md`, with ai-description as a real-world example of LLM-powered plugin functionality."}],"totalDurationMs":96634,"totalTokens":64225,"totalToolUseCount":57,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1567,"cache_read_input_tokens":58654,"output_tokens":3999,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1567},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"},"toolStats":{"readCount":39,"searchCount":1,"bashCount":17,"editFileCount":0,"linesAdded":0,"linesRemoved":0,"otherToolCount":0}}}

Latency was stable from 20:00 through 11:45 the next morning (~14 hours). Not correlated with the degradation onset.\n- **ai-description-lazy-cache gate**: This gate is ON and was associated with the v2.2 deploy. Same reasoning -- stable for 14 hours before incident.\n- **LLM model change**: litellm-proxy latency was stable until ~12:05 UTC. Its degradation onset is ~2 hours after the warehouse gate was enabled, consistent with cascading resource saturation, not an independent LLM issue.\n\n## Mitigation Applied\n\n**Action**: Disabled `warehouse-full-inventory-sync` feature gate (set state from `on` to `off`).\n**Revision**: rev-auto-38cce7c6\n**Time**: ~2026-04-17 14:00 UTC\n\nThis stops the warehouse full inventory sync at its source, removing the excessive load on `postgresql-primary`.\n\n## Team to Notify\n\n**warehouse-product** (p.warehouse@example.com) -- they own the `warehouse-full-inventory-sync` feature gate and the warehouse service that generated the unsustainable database load. Here's the summary:\n\n**Root cause**: The `warehouse-full-inventory-sync` feature gate was enabled at 10:00 UTC by the **warehouse-product** team (`p.warehouse@example.com`). It generated ~2000 RPM of `/api/products/{id}` calls, saturating the shared `postgresql-primary` database. The warehouse `db_query_rate` spiked from ~42 qpm to ~1700+ qpm, and the host-app DB connection pool rose from ~35% to ~85%+.\n\n**Impact chain**: When `ai-description` tried to fetch product data, its DB wait time jumped from <300ms to 2500ms+, pushing end-to-end p99 from ~1300ms to 4200ms+.
